1. PURPOSE
1.1 This fictional SOP defines how FTD personnel record, quarantine, report, investigate and
    disposition any temperature excursion affecting stored material.

2. SCOPE
2.1 Applies to all FTD temperature-controlled storage units: refrigerators/cold rooms (prefix CR),
    freezers (prefix FZ) and controlled room temperature rooms (prefix RM).
2.2 Applies to raw materials, intermediates and finished goods held in those units.

3. DEFINITIONS
3.1 Temperature excursion: any reading outside the defined range of a storage unit.
3.2 Magnitude: degrees Celsius (C) beyond the nearest range limit at the peak reading.
3.3 Duration: minutes from excursion start to the time the unit returns within range.
3.4 QA Temperature Review Team: the designated quality team that receives all excursion notices.
3.5 Quarantine tag: a label with ID format QT-NNNNNN applied to affected material.

4. RESPONSIBILITIES
4.1 Detecting person (any trained employee): records the excursion and applies quarantine.
4.2 QA Specialist: leads the investigation and approves disposition of MINOR excursions.
4.3 QA Head: approves disposition of MAJOR excursions; co-approves CRITICAL excursions.
4.4 Quality Director: co-approves disposition of CRITICAL excursions and must be notified of them.

5. STORAGE RANGES AND CLASSIFICATION
5.1 Defined ranges by unit type:
      REFRIGERATED (CR units)            : 2.0 C to 8.0 C
      FROZEN (FZ units)                  : -25.0 C to -15.0 C
      CONTROLLED_ROOM_TEMP (RM units)    : 15.0 C to 25.0 C
5.2 Every excursion is classified using BOTH magnitude and duration. The final class is the
    HIGHER of the two results.
5.3 Classification thresholds:
      MINOR    : magnitude <= 2.0 C            AND duration <= 60 minutes
      MAJOR    : magnitude > 2.0 C and <= 5.0 C  OR duration > 60 and <= 240 minutes
      CRITICAL : magnitude > 5.0 C             OR duration > 240 minutes

6. PROCEDURE
6.1 Record the excursion
    6.1.1 Within 15 minutes of detection, the detecting person records: excursion start date/time,
          unit ID, temperature at detection, peak temperature and the recorder's name.
    6.1.2 QA assigns an Excursion ID in the format EXC-YYYY-NNNN (sequence restarts each year).
    6.1.3 When the unit returns to range, record the end time and calculate duration in minutes.
6.2 Quarantine affected material
    6.2.1 Within 30 minutes of detection, apply a quarantine tag (QT-NNNNNN) to all affected lots.
    6.2.2 Set the inventory status of affected lots to QUARANTINED. Quarantined material must not be
          used, shipped or moved to another unit without QA approval.
6.3 Notify the designated quality team
    6.3.1 Notify the QA Temperature Review Team by phone AND email. The maximum time from detection
          to notification (the notification SLA) depends on class:
            MINOR    : within 24 hours (1440 minutes)
            MAJOR    : within 4 hours (240 minutes)
            CRITICAL : within 1 hour (60 minutes), and the Quality Director is also notified
    6.3.2 A notification later than its SLA is recorded as "Notify_SLA_Met = N".
6.4 Investigate
    6.4.1 A QA Specialist owns the investigation. It must be completed within this many calendar
          days of the excursion start:
            MINOR : 20 days      MAJOR : 10 days      CRITICAL : 5 days
    6.4.2 The investigation documents: root cause, time-temperature exposure of each lot, assessment
          against available stability data, and impact on other lots in the same unit.
6.5 Disposition decision
    6.5.1 Permitted dispositions: RELEASE, RELEASE_WITH_CONDITIONS, REJECT.
    6.5.2 MINOR  : QA Specialist decides. RELEASE is normal when stability data supports it.
    6.5.3 MAJOR  : QA Head decides.
    6.5.4 CRITICAL: default disposition is REJECT. Release (with conditions) is allowed only when
          stability data supports it AND both the QA Head and the Quality Director approve.
    6.5.5 Until a disposition is recorded, the Excursion status is OPEN and the disposition is PENDING.
6.6 Close out
    6.6.1 Remove quarantine tags only after the disposition is approved.
    6.6.2 Update the excursion log (Appendix A) and set status to CLOSED.

7. RECORDS AND RETENTION
7.1 The excursion log, investigation reports and approvals are retained for 7 years.

8. REVISION HISTORY
    Rev 01 | 2021-02-01 | Initial issue
    Rev 02 | 2023-09-01 | Added CRITICAL class and Quality Director co-approval
    Rev 03 | 2025-03-15 | Added notification SLAs and investigation due-day limits per class
